HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0a969cc05d363c89e27b","text":"4. **Exploratory: a small actionable margin.** Fixed `ي س ر/B002` is a slight amount or short duration, set beside `ع س ر/B001` difficulty. The clause is repeated in 94:6 and immediately followed by the renewed exertion of 94:7, so a limited opening can recur and be used without becoming total leisure. The smallness branch supplies the margin; repetition supplies renewal. **Abductive move:** I infer repeated small margins -> sufficient operating room for the next task. **Reading change:** **يسرا** need not be heard only as comprehensive relief; it can activate the idea of just enough space or time to move.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B006","root_001694/B001","root_001694/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1abaa0c5b9fe27af6208","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:5:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B006\"]],[\"94:5:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Doğum sancısı, açılma ve üretken artış çağrışımları, zorluğu yalnızca yok edilmesi gereken engel değil, taşınanın doğuma ulaştığı üretken basınç olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B005","root_001694/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1c3d06909197fdeded50","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:5:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B005\"]],[\"94:5:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Zorluk ve kolaylığın aynı sol tarafı çağrıştırması, rahatlamayı karşı yakaya geçmekten çok güçlük içinde farklı bir kullanma biçimi bulmak olarak düşündürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001694/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1c76000631d101da11dc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:5:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:5:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu ilk söz, önceki göğüs, yük ve bel dizisindeki müdahaleyi genelleştirerek zorluğun içinde kullanılabilir bir açılma bulunduğunu gösterir; bağımsız bir özdeyiş olmaktan çıkar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3581603d143d630f563b","text":"1. **An opening embedded in difficulty rather than postponed until after it.** Fixed `ع س ر/B001` is difficulty and fixed `ي س ر/B001` is ease or ready opening. The construction places them with one another, while 94:2-4 has already shown unloading and raising operating inside the account of strain; 94:7 turns the available opening into action. The difficulty branch supplies resistance, and the ease branch supplies a concurrently usable affordance. **Abductive move:** I infer coexistence -> an opening accessible during the difficult condition, rather than a later replacement; **مع** supplies coexistence but not the exact causal relation. **Reading change:** the statement identifies an internal route through hardship, not merely a timetable promising that hardship will end.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001694/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_382cb6b2a670bc84b8cb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:5:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:5:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kolaylığın az miktar veya kısa süre çağrışımı, büyük bir rahatlama yerine bir sonraki adımı atmaya yetecek küçük fakat yenilenen bir pay bulunduğunu düşündürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_53854f0d87278569d402","text":"3. **Labor contraction accompanied by an opening for delivery.** Fixed `ع س ر/B006` is difficult childbirth and fixed `ي س ر/B001` is readiness and opening. The window's `ر ح م/B003` womb, `و ض ع/B002` delivery, and the back-strain of 94:3 form a reproductive sequence, while 94:7 marks activity after release. The fixed branches assign contraction/obstruction and opening to the same labor interval. **Abductive move:** I infer that **مع** binds them as simultaneous phases of delivery; the packet does not name pregnancy or birth as the referent. **Reading change:** the line can describe the productive pressure by which a carried burden reaches release.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B005","root_001012/B002","root_001694/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_540f1fa087af40ee3bf1","text":"2. **A debt-and-solvency mechanism.** Fixed `ع س ر/B002` is financial straitness and `ع س ر/B003` is pressure on an insolvent debtor; fixed `ي س ر/B003` is means and prosperity. Earlier branches supply `ص د ر/B005` monetary liability, `و ض ع/B004` reduction from capital, and `و ز ر/B002` burden. The fixed hardship branches play debtor pressure, while the ease branch plays restored means. **Abductive move:** I infer that the earlier burden is debt-like and that relief rehabilitates solvency; no explicit transaction is stated. **Reading change:** hardship-with-ease becomes an economic transition from pressed liability to available means, not only general emotional relief.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7c6ebe96a652cd2e1fe7","text":"94:6 made the first pairing look less like a one-time resolution and more like a renewable relation; this especially strengthened the exploratory `ي س ر/B002` reading of recurring small margins. 94:7 then corrected any assumption that ease means lasting inactivity: its immediate completion-toil transition made the available opening operational.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B008","root_001694/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a6ed4c18ed6d62257060","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:5:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B008\"]],[\"94:5:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Daha sonraki yeni çaba, kolaylığın kalıcı dinlenme değil, zorluğun içinde oluşan ve bir sonraki işe geçmeyi mümkün kılan hazırlık olduğunu açığa çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001012/B002","root_001012/B005","root_001012/B006","root_001147/B001","root_001657/B004","root_001694/B001","root_001694/B003","root_001694/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ace65102eba7fb5b8742","text":"1. The ease is structurally with the prior bodily hardship. Lexical evidence: `ع س ر B001` difficulty/severity and `ي س ر B001` ease/readiness after hardship; earlier `و ز ر B002` heavy burden, `ن ق ض B005` creaking strain. Structural cue: the `فـ` links the statement to the preceding relief sequence. Assigned role: hardship is the field; ease is the companion capacity disclosed inside or alongside it. Abductive move supplied by me: the earlier intervention is offered as evidence for the general pattern. Reading change: the ayah reads as the logic extracted from the chest-load-back episode, not as a detached aphorism. 2. A financial/liability mechanism is activated. Lexical evidence: `ع س ر B002/B003` straitness of means and pressing an insolvent debtor; `ي س ر B003` prosperity/means; `و ض ع B004` reduction of capital or abatement; `ذ ك ر B008` rights document. Structural cue: 94:2 removes a `وزر`, and 94:4 raises a `ذكر`; together they can behave as liability lifted and status recognized. Assigned role: `عسر` is insolvency/pressure, `يسر` is restored means or lenient dealing. Abductive move supplied by me: the passage converts debt-pressure into solvency-space. Reading change: the ayah can be read as release from constricting claims into means. 3. The left-side branches produce a surprising non-triumphal ease. Lexical evidence: `ع س ر B005` the left side/left-handedness and `ي س ر B004` the left side/left hand. Structural cue: the apparent opposite roots share a lateral semantic zone; the packet repeats the pair in 94:5-6. Assigned role: the shared “left” field makes ease not simply the opposite side of hardship but a skillful handling of the same difficult side. Abductive move supplied by me: one can read `مع` as coexistence in one bodily orientation rather than replacement. Reading change: the ayah becomes about ambidexterity within constraint, not clean escape from constraint. 4. The childbirth and productive-growth reading is activated. Lexical evidence: `ع س ر B006` difficult childbirth; `ي س ر B006` milk and offspring increase; earlier `ر ح م B003/B004` womb and womb pain, `و ض ع B002` laying down pregnancy. Structural cue: the passage’s early mercy/womb and laying-down branches precede the doubled hardship/ease formula. Assigned role: `عسر` is labor difficulty, `يسر` is successful delivery and growth. Abductive move supplied by me: the burden set down in 94:2 can be reimagined as birth, so ease is not the absence of travail but its outcome and accompaniment. Reading change: the ayah reads as generative hardship. 5. A readiness-before-action reading prepares 94:7. Lexical evidence: `ي س ر B001` becoming ready/possible, `ع س ر B008` riding or taking before readiness; later `ف ر غ B001/B006` completion and deliberate turning, `ن ص ب B004` exertion. Structural cue: 94:5-6 stand between relief and the imperative to work. Assigned role: ease is readiness created inside hardship, guarding against premature coercive action. Abductive move supplied by me: the passage pauses to establish readiness before renewed labor. Reading change: ease becomes preparation for the next task, not mere rest.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B002","root_001012/B003","root_001694/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_baabd7bffe6036b8d450","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:5:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]],[\"94:5:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Darlık, ödeme baskısı ve maddi imkân çağrışımları, ilişkiyi borç altında sıkıştırılmaktan yeniden ödeme gücü ve hareket alanı kazanmaya geçiş olarak da kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B005","root_001694/B001","root_001694/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c5b1a685706d5760b938","text":"94:7 was the key later correction: ease is not an invitation to stop but readiness for `نصب`, including renewed toil. That made `ي س ر B001` read more as preparation than comfort. 94:8 also redirected the prosperity/means reading; `ي س ر B003` is not merely restored capacity in general, because desire is channeled toward `رَبِّكَ`. The left-side overlap between `ع س ر B005` and `ي س ر B004` remained the most surprising lexical activation, since it preserves coexistence rather than clean opposition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
